- **sweep-comments**: Deletes disallowed comments from the lines that the current branch added, compared with the repository's default branch, including the working tree. --base names another base branch. --widen sweeps the full content of each touched file. A list of files replaces the diff. A bundled classifier sorts every comment into a category.
